- **skill-template**: Generator-Skill — erzeugt SKILL.md-Stubs für neue Skills. Trigger durch skill-mapping-agent wenn benötigter Skill fehlt. - **skill-template**: Use when ... (replace with a one-sentence trigger description so agents can decide when to load this skill). Be specific about the task, not the implementation. ([filipmares/agent-plugins](https://github.com/filipmares/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/.templates/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: Starter template for creating a new Hermes skill. Use when the user wants a reusable skill scaffold or asks for a fresh prompt-based assistant capability. ([Fritnes/hermes-skills-assistant](https://github.com/Fritnes/hermes-skills-assistant/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: Ce que fait le skill et dans quelles situations l'utiliser. ([FTurleque/ai-toolkit-registry](https://github.com/FTurleque/ai-toolkit-registry/tree/HEAD/templates/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: Documents the canonical SKILL.md layout and refactor guide for converting rules or docs into skills. Load when the user needs format guidance, reformatting, or conversion — not when authoring a new skill from scratch. ([Gargoyle-Apps/local-model-assessor](https://github.com/Gargoyle-Apps/local-model-assessor/tree/HEAD/.skills-harness/.skills/_skills/skill-template) · 2 copies)
- **skill-template**: This template is for standalone skills that exist outside of a capability. Most skills live inside capabilities (capabilities/X/skills/), but some cross-cutting skills may be defined independently. ([GIT-PHOENIX-HUB/phoenix-toolbox](https://github.com/GIT-PHOENIX-HUB/phoenix-toolbox/tree/HEAD/templates/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: One or two sentences on what the skill does, then when to use it with the phrases a user would actually say — "do X for Y", "check Z". End with what it is not for — "Do not use for A or B." Max 1024 characters. ([gsbdarc/yen_cluster_skills](https://github.com/gsbdarc/yen_cluster_skills/tree/HEAD/templates/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: TODO — state what this skill produces AND when to activate it. Include the phrasings users actually type, in Chinese and English. The description is the only thing an agent reads before deciding whether to load this skill, so a description that omits the trigger phrasing will not activate, and one that omits the boundary will activate too often. Keep it under 1024 characters. ([guoguojenna/testing-skills](https://github.com/guoguojenna/testing-skills/tree/HEAD/templates/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: Vervang deze beschrijving. Zeg in één zin welk concreet probleem de skill oplost, en daarna wanneer een agent ze moet laden ("Gebruik wanneer iemand vraagt ..."). Houd het onder 1024 tekens. ([HansF/digitale-inclusie-lokale-overheid](https://github.com/HansF/digitale-inclusie-lokale-overheid/tree/HEAD/templates/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: **Role:** [Agent Role] **Agent:** [Agent Names] **Deterministic:** Yes — Not theoretical **Certification:** Required Week 2 ([hcindus/AOS-Brain](https://github.com/hcindus/AOS-Brain/tree/HEAD/AGI_COMPANY/shared/skills/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: REPLACE ME (1–1024 chars): state WHAT this skill does and WHEN an agent should use it, with keywords agents can match on. End with the negative trigger: "Do NOT use for ..." so the skill doesn't over-fire. ([hjmsano/ai-agent-skills](https://github.com/hjmsano/ai-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/templates/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: One or two sentences on what the skill does, then the situations and user phrases that should trigger it — include Hindi/Hinglish phrasings users actually type. State what it does NOT cover so the agent routes elsewhere. This field is the trigger; keep the "when to use" here, not in the body. ([iambeniwal/awesome-indian-legal-skills](https://github.com/iambeniwal/awesome-indian-legal-skills/tree/HEAD/templates/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: A starter skill showing the required SKILL.md frontmatter and structure. Use as a reference when creating new personal skills in this repo. Replace this description with one that explains WHEN to use the skill. ([IanLuo/skills](https://github.com/IanLuo/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-template))
- **skill-template**: To use: copy the whole `skill-template/` directory to `.claude/skills/&lt;Group&gt;/&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;/`, rename nothing (the dir name IS the skill name), fill in the bracketed parts, delete every comment block, and work the file down. Claude Code auto-discovers `.claude/skills/**/SKILL.md`; there is no registration step. ([jacgit18/Agent-Skill-Project-Skeleton](https://github.com/jacgit18/Agent-Skill-Project-Skeleton/tree/HEAD/template/skill-template))

- **skill-template**: One or two sentences on what this skill judges or produces, then "Use when …" with the words a builder would type (e.g. "qualify accounts against an ICP", "brief before a sales call").
